# SPEC-0090 - Governed offline field proof exclusive capability composition

## Status

CONTRACT REVISION 3 CANDIDATE FOR FINAL INDEPENDENT ADVERSARIAL REVIEW. Source: [ADR-0090](../adr/ADR-0090-governed-offline-field-proof-exclusive-capability-composition.md). Direction APPROVED_FOR_SPEC, not adversarially approved. Baseline main `77d482c97a663a527dcacc85036c7577dfc92782`. IMPLEMENTATION_AUTHORIZED=NO; REAL_FIELD_PROOF=HOLD. These are conceptual contracts, not executable SQL.

## 1. Terminology and trust

Binding is immutable plan/identity scope; attempt is a durable claim; generation is its monotonic fence; execution is one launcher instance's ownership. attempt_id, generation, execution_id are distinct. A protected 256-bit execution possession secret is generated before claim; only SHA-256 digest is stored. UUID/flag alone is not ownership. No secret/digest is logged, projected or reconstructed after process loss.

Existing OfflineFieldProofOutcome/AttestedCeremonyStage retain meaning. Control layers are bookkeeping; domain projections come from exact rows, not stage flags. Unknown never means zero. SCHEMA_DEPLOYMENT_ADMIN installs trusted objects; SERVICE_IDENTITY_ADMIN allocates logins/ACLs; PLAN_BINDING_ADMIN activates/recovers/records results; SECRET_DELIVERY_OPERATOR handles login secrets; OFFLINE_OPERATOR runs launcher. These need not be different humans. SINGLE_ADMIN_CAN_SELF_AUTHORIZE=POSSIBLE_UNLESS_EXTERNAL_GOVERNANCE_SEPARATES_DUTIES. Host/database superuser is trusted, outside operational attacker model, can bypass ACL/data/session authorization. Admin-proxied operational connections are procedurally forbidden; session_user cannot prove original login against hostile superuser. Trusted instances do not share execution secrets or clone process memory.

## 2. Total state machines

Exhaustive table; unlisted transitions denied. Read YES means four auditor wrappers under R, subject to authentic identity, READY incarnation and bounded read policy. ADMIN is separate reviewed administration, not offline surface. Mutation eligibility is intersection of layers. Auditor changes no layer. Terminal flags are layer-specific. Legal inbound edges are exactly reverse of listed next-state edges. Provisioning explicitly initializes records; missing record is not an initial state.

| Layer/state | Allowed wrapper class | Read | Mutation | Claim | Recovery | Next states/actor | Terminal mutation/read |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Binding REGISTERED | Auditor | YES | ADMIN activation | NO | ADMIN inspection | ACTIVE/REVOKED/EXPIRED by ADMIN/time | NO/NO |
| Binding ACTIVE | Auditor; eligible effects | YES | M/other layers | Initial/governed recovered claim | ADMIN | REVOKED/EXPIRED by ADMIN/time | NO/NO |
| Binding REVOKED | Auditor | YES | NO | NO | Read/classify | None | YES/NO |
| Binding EXPIRED | Auditor | YES | NO | NO | Read/classify | None | YES/NO |
| Attempt CLAIMED | Auditor; eligible V/I/E | YES | M | Original ownership ack retry only | ADMIN drain | EFFECTS_IN_PROGRESS on first committed stage receipt; RECOVERY_REQUIRED/ABORTED by ADMIN | NO/NO |
| Attempt EFFECTS_IN_PROGRESS | Auditor; eligible V/I/E | YES | M/stage order | NO new claim | ADMIN drain | EFFECTS_COMPLETE on decision commit; RECOVERY_REQUIRED/ABORTED by ADMIN | NO/NO |
| Attempt RECOVERY_REQUIRED | Auditor | YES | NO offline effects | NO | ADMIN classify | ABORTED after drain; EFFECTS_COMPLETE after exact existing decision/head | YES/NO |

| Execution STALE | Auditor | YES | NO | NO | Read/classify | None | YES/NO |

| Delivery DELIVERY_FAILED_REVIEW_REQUIRED | Auditor | YES | NO continuation | NO | ADMIN manual review | None | YES/NO |
| Reconciliation NOT_STARTED | Auditor; pre-decision effects | YES | Eligible stages/M | Attempt rules | ADMIN | REQUIRED at decision commit/recovery discovery | NO/NO |
| Reconciliation REQUIRED | Auditor | YES | NO new decision | NO | ADMIN | IN_PROGRESS/RECONCILED/MISMATCH/INDETERMINATE by evidence recorder | YES/NO |

DELIVERY_AUTHORIZED is transient inside delivery-consume, immediately committed as DELIVERY_ATTEMPTED plus consumed permission. Never a separately committed replayable authorization.

Domain projection: EMPTY -> ACCEPTED_ONLY -> PRINCIPAL_ONLY_EXACT -> CREDENTIAL_PRESENT -> GRANT_PRESENT -> DECISION_HEAD_PRESENT. Existing accepted evidence can make first verification an exact read/replay rather than creation. Each effect requires frozen receipt; accepted evidence alone is not current authority. Mismatch/unknown are read classifications, not caller-set domain statuses. Early recovery permits EMPTY/ACCEPTED_ONLY/PRINCIPAL_ONLY_EXACT only.

Decision APPLY atomically writes decision/head, consumes admission, sets EFFECTS_COMPLETE/RELEASED and reconciliation REQUIRED; ceremony remains NONE. Formal recovery: RECOVERY_REQUIRED + exact committed decision/head -> ADMIN drains/records EFFECTS_COMPLETE/RELEASED/REQUIRED -> bound auditor reads/JVM verifies -> separate ADMIN evidence recorder commits RECONCILED+SUCCESS, MISMATCH+FAILURE or INDETERMINATE+MANUAL_REVIEW. Recorder independently recomputes bound durable evidence, canonical/JCA proof and head, never trusts caller boolean; writes control evidence/result only. IN_PROGRESS is optional administrative start record; REQUIRED may directly reach proven result. Before-decision proven failure/uncertainty may ADMIN-record FAILURE/MANUAL_REVIEW. If a terminal result is already recorded, later reconciliation records diagnostic evidence without rewriting that historical terminal result or reopening mutation. Effect completion never equals reconciled success. Every state remains readable by same bound auditor, no post-expiry/revoke read authorization needed.

## 3. Identity and guards

Four distinct LOGIN NOINHERIT VERIFIER/ISSUER/EXECUTOR/AUDITOR; zero direct/transitive memberships; NOSUPERUSER/NOCREATEROLE/NOCREATEDB/NOREPLICATION/NOBYPASSRLS; no ownership/CREATE/grant option/shared runtime/issuer/verifier/governance/owner roles. SERVICE LOGINS: NO RAW TABLE ACCESS, including control/sequences. Only exact assigned EXECUTE plus minimum CONNECT/USAGE. Pools dedicated per login/incarnation; prepared statements reexecute guards, never cache authority. Catalog facilities are not new tenant-data capabilities; preflight audits all reachable application ACL paths.

R=READ_RECONCILIATION_GUARD: server READY incarnation matches header/client expectation; session_user catalog OID+expected name identifies AUDITOR; B fields/fingerprint match header; all data exact plan. Allows every state, including revoked/expired/terminal. Ignores mutation/admission expiry but enforces read policy; no effect locks/writes/claim/reactivation/renewal. Disabled login/rename/recreation/incarnation mismatch fails. R never reopens M.

M=MUTATION_GUARD: authentic exact effect slot; immutable scope/READY incarnation; ACTIVE and valid_from <= DB wall clock < expires_at; ceremony NONE; eligible attempt/delivery/reconciliation stage; current attempt/generation/execution/instance, OWNED execution and possession-secret digest, valid absolute attempt/execution deadlines. Lock lifecycle -> current-pointer/attempt -> execution -> relevant delivery/admission FOR UPDATE, retain until commit. Lock-column UPDATE permits locks only. Check before domain lookup; repeat current generation/execution/stage/time/canonical authority before final effect. After any frozen mutation returns, recheck DB wall clock and unchanged execution/deadline before returning its receipt; expiration or mismatch raises an error and rolls back that transaction including the tentative domain write. Trusted adapters roll back on any wrapper error, never commit partial stages. Eligibility concerns canonical write time, not visibility at later client COMMIT; an effect admitted and written within the window can become visible after expiry, as with serialized revocation. No later new effect is authorized. Claim bootstrap requires no live execution and administratively permitted claim.

POSSESSION_AUTHORITY=GUARD_M. POSSESSION_VALIDITY_FOR_MUTATION is established exclusively by guard M at each mutable-operation time, using the supplied execution possession secret against the stored digest and the current immutable organization/binding, deployment/incarnation, attempt/generation, execution/instance, authentic effect slot and exact operation context. For admission-consuming operations, the exact admission_id and its organization/executor/authority tuple must also match; pre-admission stages retain their existing guards and claim bootstrap exception, without fabricating an admission. Missing proof/context, stale execution/generation/deadline or any mismatch denies the operation before effect; failed final revalidation rolls back tentative effects. Admission history, an ISSUED row, OWNED state, UUID or earlier successful validation never substitutes for current possession. No new possession receipt/subsystem is introduced.

Preflight returns bounded authenticated non-secret scope receipt without DB writes, using HMAC-SHA-256 and a private deployment key inaccessible to service logins. Receipt encoding uses the section 4 tagged framing under domain FLOOOW/OFFLINE-FIELD-PROOF/PREFLIGHT/V1, tags 1-8 for incarnation UUID, binding fingerprint hash32, surface UTF-8, history digest hash32, ACL digest hash32, policy digest hash32, issued_at instant and expires_at instant. MAC input includes positive u32 deployment key version as tag 9; output is versioned receipt bytes plus 32-byte MAC. Client does not receive key or create authenticated receipts. Covers incarnation/binding fingerprint/surface/full history digest/ACL+policy digest/issue+expiry. Claim verifies and rechecks live catalog/history/readiness; caller UUID/flag cannot forge PASS. Receipt is administrative bootstrap, not command authority/JCA proof. Key provisioning is trusted deployment work, separate from signed canonical manifest authority. Only Q reads active key version/material and readiness configuration; A/E invoke the restricted internal readiness capability and have no key SELECT. Administrative owner alone rotates it. The implementation uses a vetted HMAC primitive (a reviewed extension dependency only if necessary), not a new cryptographic algorithm. Section21.2 fixes the complete digest projections/encodings; generated golden evidence remains required before implementation freeze/activation.

NEW_EFFECT_PATH uses M/admission. REPLAY_INSPECTION_PATH uses auditor R, no admission/effects. Completed-decision AlreadyApplied is exact read result only. Live frozen stage AlreadyApplied never restores execution/delivery eligibility. Closed executor mutation rejects with REPLAY_INSPECTION_REQUIRED. Consumed admission remains consumed.

## 4. Immutable binding and fingerprint

Header contains all tags below plus fingerprint, separately from mutable layers. Permanent uniqueness (incarnation,plan_id), binding_id and login allocation; tombstones forbid reuse/rebinding. Distinct OIDs/names, canonical manifest decode/re-encode/digest agreement. Restore never remaps headers.

DOMAIN_SEPARATOR=FLOOOW/OFFLINE-FIELD-PROOF/BINDING/V1. binding_plan_fingerprint_v1 = lowercase hex SHA-256 of `u32be(domain UTF-8 byte length) || domain UTF-8 || u16be(field count) || fields ascending by tag`. Field=`u16be(tag)||u32be(payload length)||payload`. Payload null=`00`; present=`01||canonical value bytes`. Null vs empty text differ (00 vs 01). Unknown/duplicate/omitted/reordered tags rejected. No defaults/normalization. Exclude fingerprint itself and mutable states/secrets, independently guarded.

| Tags | Fields exactly in listed order | Types |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1-7 | binding_schema_version,binding_id,deployment_id,deployment_incarnation_id,run_id,plan_id,execution_plan_version | u32,UUID x5,u32 |
| 8-12 | organization_id,manifest_id,manifest_digest,canonical_manifest_hash,canonical_manifest_encoding_version | UUID x2,hash32 x2,u32 |
| 13-18 | mercado_livre_connection_id,omie_connection_id,marketplace_order_id,source_order_reference,integration_reference,permission | UUID x3,text,text,enum |
| 19-25 | reason,provenance,principal_id,credential_id,grant_id,decision_id,correlation_id | text x2,UUID x5 |
| 26-28 | principal_operation_id,credential_operation_id,grant_operation_id | UUID x3 |
| 29-33 | issued_at,valid_from,expires_at,identity_slots,offline_surface_version | instant x3,slot collection,text |
| 34-38 | deadline_policy_version,deadline_policy_digest,admission_contract_version,delivery_contract_version,reconciliation_contract_version | text,hash32,text x3 |

V1 count=38. All 38 fields are required and non-null. integration_reference is non-null, matching ApprovalManifest.integrationReference; optional/null framing is defined for codec discrimination, not permission to weaken this header. canonical_manifest_hash=SHA-256 exact retained bytes; manifest_digest=validated lowercase 64-hex decoded to 32 bytes. Existing source_order_reference/integration_reference/marketplace_order_id define target, no invented source-order UUID. canonical bytes also bind signed policy claims.

UUID=16 network-order bytes (most-significant then least-significant 64 bits), independent of text. u32=four unsigned network-order bytes, versions positive/OID range checked. Instant=signed i64 network-order microseconds since epoch; reject sub-microsecond input. Text=strict UTF-8 already NFC plus existing field control/empty rules; reject non-NFC, do not normalize. Enums=exact existing ASCII names, no ordinal/coercion. Future booleans=one-byte 00/01.

Slots=u32 count 4 then u32 byte-length-prefixed records sorted purposes VERIFIER=1/ISSUER=2/EXECUTOR=3/AUDITOR=4; record=purpose u8+OID u32+name u32 byte length/UTF-8. Missing/duplicate purpose or OID/name invalid. General ordered-list encoding=u32 count/u32 byte-length-prefixed elements preserving order; sets sorted canonical element bytes/duplicates rejected. V1 has only fixed slot collection, no arbitrary extensible lists. Role attribute checks are live.

PLAN_FINGERPRINT_VERSIONED=YES; DOMAIN_SEPARATED=YES. Existing OfflineFieldProofExecutionPlan.fingerprint() stays separate legacy ID digest, never accepted as binding fingerprint. Require legacy execution-plan version/non-nil/distinct-ID validation and canonical manifest field constraints before hashing. Mandatory independent JVM/reference codec vectors: NFC Unicode accepted/non-NFC rejected; null/empty codec encodings unequal and null header fields rejected; missing/duplicate/reordered tags rejected; UUID text variants same bytes; every one of 38 fields mutated changes digest or invalidates input; reordered ordered-list changes bytes; shuffled slot-set input sorts identically/duplicates rejected; organization/manifest/target/correlation/operation mutation denied. Normative fixtures21.2 and generated golden evidence before implementation freeze are mandatory; no generated golden approval is claimed now.

## 5. Incarnation and storage

Separate header/lifecycle/attempt/execution/delivery/admission/reconciliation/evidence/result records, FKs/uniqueness and append-only recovery/incident lineage. Wrapper owners never own tables. Receipts/current locks authorize listed control writes, not arbitrary caller statuses. Result evidence contains snapshot digest/version/recorder/time, no secrets.

NOT_READY/READY is host-governed. Clone/restore starts NOT_READY before exposure: block operational network/authentication, disable restored offline logins, explicit REINCARNATION with fresh identity governed outside ordinary DB backup, rotate private DB incarnation marker, invalidate copied headers, verify history/catalog/watchdog, then enable freshly reviewed identities/bindings. Old identities tombstoned/disabled, never rebound. Existing credential/later effects -> manual review, no regeneration. READY requires host incarnation/private marker agreement. Client expected endpoint/incarnation from approved deployment config, never copied header. DB cannot detect undeclared clone if trusted host admin bypasses procedure; hostile-admin protection not claimed. Same names/OIDs/content copied fixture must deny while NOT_READY and after new incarnation. Normal restart retains incarnation and ownership.

## 6. Attempt/execution exclusion

Launcher allocates instance/execution ID and random 32-byte secret before claim. Atomic claim locks current control pointer, verifies preflight/live readiness, creates server attempt ID at administratively allocated generation plus one OWNED execution/digest/deadline, commits. Same-login second process -> EXECUTION_ALREADY_OWNED unless identical original instance/execution/secret/request proved. Original lost-ack retry changes nothing, never renews deadline/delivery permission. IDs alone insufficient.

Every mutable V/I/E call carries execution proof through four dedicated data sources in same process. Control locks serialize stages; trusted adapter executes one at a time and never duplicates identity into another process. Second process cannot obtain proof from DB. Compromised/cloned process memory outside trusted-instance model. Absolute deadlines, no automatic renewal.

Crash/disconnect/timeout never releases ownership. ADMIN drain/cancel old sessions, take locks, mark STALE/ABORTED with evidence, increment generation atomically, permit new claim only for early exact state/ceremony NONE. Existing decision/head -> reconcile instead. Old admission/proof/prepared statement/final APPLY fail. No wrapper advances generation/activates/rebinds/extends validity. Concurrent administrators serialize current pointer.

## 7. Owner ACL and principal lock

V/I/E/A owners (plus internal P/Q/Z specified in21.7) separate NOLOGIN NOINHERIT NOSUPERUSER NOCREATEROLE NOCREATEDB NOREPLICATION NOBYPASSRLS, zero memberships, wrappers only. ADMIN owner owns controls. Additional internal lock owner P has same protections, owns principal lock only; not fifth service identity. No retained unnecessary CREATE/grant options/PUBLIC access.

| Owner | Reads/locks | Control writes | Exact capabilities/domain writes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| V | Header/incarnation/policy/lifecycle/attempt/execution; lock-column UPDATE | Verification receipt/progress after accepted receipt | Two V041 BEGIN/PERSIST; no direct domain DML |
| I | Same plus delivery stage; lock-column UPDATE | Principal/credential/grant receipts; credential delivery initialization/originating execution; progress | Six V042 issuance functions; no direct domain DML |
| E | Same plus admission/delivery and execution projection below; lock-column UPDATE | Claim at unchanged generation; admission insert/consume; delivery consume/report; decision receipt/EFFECTS_COMPLETE/RELEASED/REQUIRED | V042 decision BEGIN/APPLY; bound principal/org/progress locks/pure helpers; no domain DML |
| A | Scoped audit/control/history columns in22.4; catalog readiness via Q | NONE, no effect lock privilege | Q/Z and pure helpers only |
| P | Header/control/exec guard and exact principal identity/connections; control lock-column UPDATE; SELECT plus UPDATE(principal_id) solely for FOR UPDATE | NONE | Internal bound principal lock and organization-lock EXECUTE only |

No wrapper changes header/incarnation/policy/activation/revocation/generation/expiry/reconciliation outcomes/ceremony result. Receipt-based control transitions enforce listed field changes. Lock-column UPDATE never semantic header change. P body executes no DML; existing principal immutability trigger/inaccessible owner prevent a mutation API. Sections22.4/21.7 fix respectively the exact column/control and frozen EXECUTE allowlists; no implementation may widen them without reviewed contract change.

Audit and execution privileges are exclusively22.4 per-column rows. A uses Q/Z rather than private catalog/key/verifier reads; broader same-plan revision/operation counts remain. E reads only AU/TL/OE/HD/AF/IF/DF facts needed for S13-S18; no source payload/monetary grant. V/I accepted/signer checks stay in frozen capabilities. No service internal/shared/governance/legacy-unlinked/withdrawal grant.

PRINCIPAL_LOCK_CAPABILITY_REQUIRED=YES. New internal offline_lock_bound_principal(B,T,E), P-owned SECURITY DEFINER, search_path=pg_catalog,pg_temp, no PUBLIC EXECUTE, only E EXECUTE. Independently authentic executor/M/current control checks, derive org/principal/connections from header, existing org lock then exact principal FOR UPDATE, validate pair, return one boolean/no arbitrary data. No caller-selected org/principal arguments, no DML, same effect transaction. P needs column UPDATE for this SQL lock, not E/service.

E must not directly call SECURITY INVOKER transaction_identity_locks requiring principal UPDATE. Reproduce traced order through bound helper/progress/advisory locks. Frozen V042 later reenters invoker under unchanged capability owner. No frozen function/security/table changes.

## 8. Complete wrapper contracts

16 names, three typed claim overloads:18 public SQL signatures fully expanded in21.1. B/T/E below are explanatory abbreviations only, not deferred physical vectors. All SECURITY DEFINER fixed search_path=pg_catalog,pg_temp, qualified objects/types, no dynamic selector. Exact-signature EXECUTE matching slot only; PUBLIC/others none. A=STABLE READ ONLY REPEATABLE READ/rollback; V/I/E=VOLATILE explicit READ COMMITTED/caller commit. No internal COMMIT. B=binding ID/fingerprint/expected incarnation/surface; T=attempt/generation; E=execution ID/instance/protected proof. Redundant fields match header before lookup. One-row outputs except history. C=section 3 control lock sequence; errors section 12.

### Three bounded claim signatures

Trusted typed requests distinguish overloads, no generic operation dispatcher. EXECUTOR/E only, VOLATILE READ COMMITTED/exact ACL. Preflight inventories all 18 signatures.

1. Claim(B,expected_generation,execution_id,instance_id,possession_secret,preflight_receipt) -> server attempt/execution/deadline. Initial M exception; original ack retry changes nothing; other instance denied; no digest output.
2. Delivery-consume(B,T,E,delivery_attempt_request): exact credential/initial-operation/original fresh Applied receipt. M/EFFECTS_IN_PROGRESS/CREATED_NOT_DELIVERABLE/same live origin. Atomically persist DELIVERY_ATTEMPTED+execution/instance/time/deadline and consume unique permission; caller commit before TTY. Repeat -> DELIVERY_ALREADY_ATTEMPTED with no permission. Lost commit ack never permits TTY/retry-to-deliver.
3. Delivery-report(B,T,E,delivery_outcome_report): exact delivery receipt+ACKNOWLEDGED/FAILED/UNKNOWN, no token. M/same execution/ATTEMPTED -> one state receipt. An identical outcome repeat returns its recorded receipt only while execution remains admissible; closed/stale/failed paths use auditor R. Conflicting report is incident/manual review, no reauthorization. Expired/revoked/stale execution cannot report via M; ADMIN classifies uncertainty without resuming effects.

TTY occurs outside SQL once after acknowledged first consumption commit in that live instance; enforce DB-issued operation window via conservative monotonic local deadline, never retry I/O. Crash before commit -> no TTY; after commit before/during TTY -> unknown/manual review/no redelivery. Grant/auth require ACKNOWLEDGED. Administrative acknowledgment after process loss never permits continuation. POSTGRESQL CANNOT PROVE HUMAN RECEIPT; ACKNOWLEDGED is operational process bookkeeping.

## 9. Durable admission

ADMISSION_DURABLE_OR_TRANSACTION_LOCAL=DURABLE. Reuse credential parser/CommandCredentialVerifier.fromCredential derivation. Derived proof is secret-equivalent protected parameter; no raw token SQL/literal/logged argument/persisted proof/returned secret_verifier. Validate latest exact credential revision, enabled principal/credential/grant, active org, connections/permission/execution and canonical eligibility.

Private receipt: server-random ref,deployment/incarnation/binding/attempt/generation/execution/instance/executor OID,credential ID/revision,principal/org,grant ID/revision/authorization fingerprint,permission,authenticated_at/expiry/durable state ISSUED or CONSUMED/consumed decision. UUID/flag alone never admission; caller cannot insert/mark valid. Changed authority revision never reuses receipt. Expiry=min(policy,attempt/execution/binding/canonical windows). APPLY revalidates current authority even if durable state ISSUED and effective validity holds and atomically consumes receipt with decision; rollback rolls both back. Closed replay R needs no admission; consumed never reactivated.

Commit admission, destroy command credential/verifier/proof buffers, then writer. Execution secret has separate protected lifetime until effects stop. Actor uses reviewed opaque admission-backed adapter/factory, never caller-UUID/boolean AuthenticatedCommand. Ordinary SQL bytea equality alone is not a constant-time claim.

## 10. Transactions and actual lock parity

BEGIN_JCA_APPLY_ENFORCEMENT=ADAPTER_TRUSTED_SEQUENCE. No new BEGIN provenance row/database receipt. Same JDBC connection/READ COMMITTED transaction: V041 BEGIN/immutable snapshot/JVM canonical-JCA/PERSIST/commit; each V042 issuer BEGIN/JCA/APPLY/independent commit; execution prepare/V042 BEGIN/JCA/APPLY/commit. Frozen PERSIST/APPLY reexecute/revalidate snapshots/fingerprints under unchanged locks. SQL does not prove honest JCA; adapter does. Tests assert continuity and interrupt/change authorized fixtures between phases to prove rollback/revalidation denial. No synthetic trust flag or SQL-only preceding-BEGIN assertion.

Flow: bootstrap -> auditor preflight/history/org/target -> inspect/replay -> confirmation -> claim/execution commit -> verifier commit -> principal commit -> credential/delivery-state commit -> delivery-consume commit -> one TTY -> report commit -> grant commit -> admission commit -> destroy command secrets -> decision transaction -> auditor reconcile -> separate ADMIN evidence/result recording. No global ceremony atomicity/provider/network/operator interaction while effect locks held.

Actual confirmed order from PostgresCommandAuthorization/PostgresTransactionIdentityWriter/V039: organization FOR SHARE; principal FOR UPDATE; current credential then grant reads/fingerprint; decision-ID advisory and existing-decision read; transaction_identity_locks reenters org/principal/decision then Omie V3 progress FOR UPDATE and lexicographically sorted subject/target advisories; target/Omie evidence/currentness; head/predecessor read; second authorization; attested BEGIN/JCA/APPLY. No invented credential/grant row locks.

New executor prefixes C plus admission lock as relevant, then same domain order through bound principal helper, private authorization reads, decision advisory, existing progress lock, sorted subject/target, evidence/head, second authorization, frozen BEGIN/JCA/APPLY. E never directly runs principal FOR UPDATE/invoker transaction_identity_locks. V042 reenters these locks under unchanged owner, then attestation-manifest advisory, accepted/consumption snapshot and signer-key/signer-authority advisory/currentness. Preserve actual nesting, not guessed categories. V/I prefix C then unchanged frozen order. ADMIN takes C before bound domain effect locks; auditor takes none. Existing head trigger/capability remains authoritative; no head UPDATE grant.

Mixed normal-runtime/offline concurrency tests prove parity/no inversion with barriers. Normal runtime never acquires new C locks. Revoke conflicts with lifecycle FOR UPDATE; already admitted effects may commit first, no retroactive rollback. Emergency cancel/drain/reconcile required. Final DB-clock time/execution/authority checks precede effect; watchdog bounds JCA gap.

## 11. Idempotency and delivery irreversibility

Fixed plan IDs/existing canonical/intent/receipt/evidence fingerprints remain mandatory. Differing payload never AlreadyApplied. Control counts excluded from successful domain counts: one accepted/consumption/principal/credential/grant/decision/head and three authority operations. Historical acceptance is not eligibility.

AT_MOST_ONE_DELIVERY_ATTEMPT, never exactly-once human receipt. Fresh credential Applied belongs to originating execution. AlreadyApplied, restart, lost acknowledgment, recovery/new generation, operator choice or terminal read never permits delivery/replacement credential. Durable consumption survives restart. Matching claim retry renews no deadline/permission; consumed admission is no replay bypass. Exact existing decision/head -> R only.

## 12. Errors

ACL denial/uniform ACCESS_DENIED for wrong identity/slot/binding/incarnation/plan/manifest/IDs/attempt/execution/proof/admission/mixed scope before foreign lookup. Existing/nonexistent B artifacts have equivalent public category/fields. Redact DETAIL/HINT/CONTEXT/parameters/cast/constraint disclosure. Only after authentic bound scope distinguish DEPLOYMENT_NOT_READY,SURFACE_MISMATCH,BINDING_NOT_ACTIVE/EXPIRED/REVOKED,EXECUTION_ALREADY_OWNED,ATTEMPT_NOT_CLAIMED,FENCING_MISMATCH,EXECUTION_STALE,DELIVERY_ALREADY_ATTEMPTED,DELIVERY_REVIEW_REQUIRED,ADMISSION_INVALID,AUTHORIZED_STATE_MISMATCH,REPLAY_INSPECTION_REQUIRED,RECONCILIATION_MISMATCH or local PLAN_MISMATCH. No foreign existence claim. Sanitized authorized SQLSTATE/object evidence, unknown stays unknown.

## 13. Two-org/two-process invariants

A/plan A/four logins and B/plan B/four logins. Deny raw SELECT even own tables, arbitrary org/progress/principal locks, foreign accepted/consumption/signer/operation/principal/credential/grant/decision/head/evidence/connection, mixed manifests/IDs, other slots/shared/governance/internal/pure functions, SET ROLE/transitive/PUBLIC paths. Foreign binding/attempt/admission/execution/proof/instance/delivery receipt/target/fingerprint denied before disclosure/effect. Generation belongs to tuple; equal integers across plans are not identities.

Same login/distinct instances concurrent claim -> one OWNED; loser cannot fetch secret/join/consume delivery. Original lost-ack claim retry idempotent; other instance denied. Concurrent delivery consumption -> one usable first receipt; repeats never TTY. Generation 2 recovery -> stale proof/admission/prepared statement/final APPLY denied. Compare domain/control before/after; positive A passes. Compromised/cloned process memory excluded explicitly.

## 14. History and compatibility

Return installed_rank/version/type/script/checksum/success only, all rows installed_rank order, no cap/filter/truncation. Reject missing/duplicate/extra/failed/null/changed checksum/script/type/order/version. Preserve V001-V042 CRC/script semantics; matching new client appends future exact version/script/checksum. Never conceal later migration.

| Client/DB | Offline behavior |
| --- | --- |
| OLD+V042 | Legacy path outside Package 0090; old prerequisites. |
| OLD+NEW | FAIL CLOSED: current old history rejects extra migration/incompatible surface. |
| NEW+V042 | FAIL CLOSED before effects: surface/history/incarnation absent. |
| NEW+NEW | Exact surface/history/binding/policy versions and preflight PASS only. |

ROLLING_UPGRADE_SUPPORTED=NO for offline ceremony. DB/package -> identities/bindings -> matching launcher; no mixed ceremony versions. Normal runtime compatibility separate. Exact installed signatures/manifest, not latest-version flag.

## 15. Migration and schema hijack

Future forward migration only: controls,16 names/18 public signatures,scalar delivery overloads,narrow principal lock/internal readiness/intent audit and separately approved private vetted HMAC dependency. No V043 created/reserved here. V001-V042 immutable; existing runtime table schema/RLS/shared roles/function ownership/security unchanged. No corrective old-launcher grants/broad ALL/ownership/CREATE/default shared grants.

PUBLIC_SCHEMA_CREATE_STATE_MUST_BE_PROVEN_BEFORE_IMPLEMENTATION_GATE. Audit service CREATE in trusted/resolvable schemas, owner unnecessary CREATE, PUBLIC CREATE in trusted-object schema, qualified objects/types/overload resolution, search_path=pg_catalog,pg_temp. PUBLIC EXECUTE removed in same future creation transaction. Unsafe existing ACL blocks gate for separately governed remediation; no frozen edit/authorized REVOKE now. Caller cannot replace trusted functions/types; qualified references cannot be temp-hijacked. Proof includes default ACL/USAGE/ownership/all 18 signatures.

## 16. Minimal bootstrap and provisioning

OFFLINE_SURFACE_VERSION=0090-v1. Before preflight client trusts approved endpoint/TLS connection where applicable,expected incarnation,exact known wrapper names/signatures/version. Policy/history manifests shipped with matching client. Never infer expectations from copied/server data or fallback raw SQL/legacy. Missing wrapper/connection/version mismatch fails closed before effects.

Preflight proves incarnation,surface,full history,four identities/attributes/memberships,18 signature ACL/owners/internal/PUBLIC/default/schema contract,binding/fingerprint,policy/watchdog,org/connections/target. Return bounded authenticated receipt; claim rechecks current readiness. Wrapper invocation alone does not establish authenticity against hostile admin; trusted installation is prerequisite. No circular raw SELECT/membership dependency.

Provision private incarnation/policy/receipt key/watchdog -> owners/controls/wrappers -> four fresh logins/header/initial states/ACLs -> activation -> matching launcher. Service password delivery distinct from command TTY. OID/name/incarnation checks/tombstones/no rebinding. Dedicated pools discard stale-incarnation sessions. No admin SET ROLE shortcut or impossible hostile-superuser session-authorization detection claim.

## 17. Restart and recovery

| Durable state | Allowed action | Forbidden | Required evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| EMPTY/ACCEPTED_ONLY | ADMIN drain/abort,higher generation,same IDs | Auto release/rebinding | Exact early state/accepted proof/old execution exclusion |
| PRINCIPAL_ONLY_EXACT | Same governed continuation | New root/operation IDs | Exact consumption/root/receipt,no credential |
| Credential before delivery consumption | MANUAL_REVIEW/read | Restart delivery/new credential/auto resume | Credential/originating receipt,no decision |
| ATTEMPTED/ACKNOWLEDGED/UNKNOWN/FAILED without decision | MANUAL_REVIEW | TTY retry/human receipt inference/auto resume | Consumed permission/incident evidence |
| Grant/admission without decision after crash | MANUAL_REVIEW | Regenerate/deliver/reuse admission | Authority/control lineage/delivery uncertainty |
| Exact decision/head | EFFECTS_COMPLETE+REQUIRED,R,ADMIN result | Reissue/new decision | Canonical/JCA/accepted/consumption/evidence/head |
| Mismatch/unknown/ambiguous commit | R/incident/manual review | Infer zero/completeness/destructive repair | Consistent durable evidence |
| Revoked/expired/terminal | R always for bound auditor | Reopen claim/mutation | Authentic identity/incarnation/read policy |

DB restart persists header/OIDs/layers/attempt/generation/execution/delivery/admission/reconciliation/result; never refreshes deadline/ownership. App restart reads durable R projection, cannot recover secret from DB. Higher generation needs ADMIN/eligible early state. Terminal results close mutation but remain readable while identity/incarnation valid; retention separately governed. ADMIN acknowledgment after credential process loss never enables automatic continuation.

## 18. Deadlines and observability

TIME_SOURCE=DATABASE_CLOCK_FOR_DATABASE_AUTHORIZATION. Approved immutable policy version/digest contains explicit finite positive values AND authorized maxima: TRANSACTION_TIMEOUT,STATEMENT_TIMEOUT,IDLE_IN_TRANSACTION_TIMEOUT,LOCK_TIMEOUT,ATTEMPT_VALIDITY,EXECUTION_VALIDITY,BINDING_VALIDITY,ADMISSION_VALIDITY,DELIVERY_OPERATION_WINDOW,read transaction duration. Reject absent/zero/unbounded/overflow/above-max. No arbitrary global seconds chosen; activation requires numeric policy evidence, fingerprinted policy digest.

G3F.3B.1 adds required server-owned policy parameter `preflight_receipt_ttl`, with its explicitly approved maximum, both positive finite int8 microsecond durations. None of the existing transaction/read/attempt/execution/binding/admission/delivery/watchdog durations is approved as the preflight receipt lifetime; no numeric TTL is chosen by this amendment. Trusted administrative policy provisioning supplies both actual and maximum AFTER V043 policy schema creation and BEFORE any policy-dependent Package0090 wrapper use, under section25. Missing values leave POLICY_BOOTSTRAP_STATE=UNPROVISIONED and deny policy-dependent wrapper use; they are not a schema-migration precondition failure or an implementation default. Policy rows are immutable by version: a changed TTL/maximum requires a newly approved policy_version and digest, never in-place mutation or receipt renewal. Neither caller input nor caller session settings determine this parameter.

Attempt/execution <= binding expiry; admission <= attempt/execution/binding/canonical windows; delivery <= execution/binding; lock/statement/idle <= transaction max. M verifies effective policy/config/DB clock on entry/final effect. Preflight proves policy/defaults/watchdog; R bounded reads ignore historical mutation expiry.

Defaults alone cannot bound external JVM gap. Trusted host/database watchdog monitors all service backends by OID/incarnation whitelist,not caller application_name; cancels/terminates transactions/idle beyond approved max with finite reviewed enforcement interval. No operational readiness without watchdog; failure blocks new claims/effect admission and triggers drain. Wrappers verify protected health marker/elapsed transaction eligibility. Caller SET timeout=0 cannot bypass independent watchdog. No offline superuser privilege/API. Revoke bound includes enforcement/drain interval,not instant cancellation.

Tests use administrative validity data/short approved policies,barriers/latches/observed locks/bounded DB-clock polling with deadlines,never brittle fixed sleeps. Test expiry either side of effect,stalled JCA/watchdog kill,caller timeout override,revoke/recovery concurrency,read after expiry. JVM Clock serves local UX,not SQL expiry authority.

Log sanitized binding/run/attempt/generation/execution/slot/stage/result/time only. No passwords/tokens/derived proof/secret_verifier/execution secret/private key/SQL credential args/TTY contents. Driver/proxy/server parameter leakage disabled. Bound JCA projections only. Acknowledgments are operational observations,not canonical truth.

Normal runtime code/schema/shared roles unchanged. Future offline adapters use four data sources/new surface,not existing raw-select adapters under restricted logins. Preserve target joins,broader lineage counts,JCA/signer windows/consumption/intent receipts/Omie page-currentness/evidence-head/final authorization via reviewed algorithm/projection parity.

Residual paths: CLI/local checks/confirmation/secret generation/JCA/destruction/one TTY remain process responsibilities. Catalog/schema/org/target -> preflight; history -> read_history; counts/lineage/replay -> inspect/reconcile; verification -> two V041 wrappers; issuance -> six V042 wrappers; credential query -> admission; authorization/principal/progress/advisory/evidence/head -> prepare/internal lock; attested BEGIN/JCA/APPLY -> prepare/JVM/apply; claim/delivery/report -> bounded claim overloads. No service raw table/catalog-readiness query/internal EXECUTE required. ADMIN recording/recovery/incarnation separate,not extra public wrapper name. No global RLS/Room writer/provider-financial authority/generic admin-query dispatcher/arbitrary audit/destructive recovery. Bookkeeping is never authority proof.

## 21. Revision 2 normative closure

This section records Revision2 closure of R1-H01/H02/H03/H04/M01/M02. Section22 is the normative Revision3 replacement for the four reviewed gaps and supersedes conflicting representation/grant statements above. It preserves all accepted architectural decisions. The specified future objects are not installed by this document. OWNER_TRANSITIVE_AUTHORITY=NOT_PROVEN_PENDING_INDEPENDENT_REVIEW.

### 21.1 Exact public SQL manifest

The 18 signatures below fully expand B/T/E. SQL aliases are exact pg_catalog types: uuid, bytea, text, int8 (bigint), int4 (integer), timestamptz, bool. No new domain/composite/JSON/polymorphic types. IDs are non-nil uuid; generation positive int8 with checked increment/no wrap; fingerprints/digests raw 32-byte bytea; surface exact text `0090-v1`; execution possession secret and derived credential proof separate protected 32-byte bytea parameters. No secret/proof/private possession digest is returned by an audit projection; public binding/history/ACL/policy digests remain specified receipt fields.

Every SQL argument is contract-NOT-NULL, mandatory, DEFAULT_ALLOWED=NO, VARIADIC=NO. Use CALLED ON NULL INPUT, not STRICT: reject null before privileged effect, never silently return null/zero rows. S01-S04 STABLE; S05-S18 VOLATILE. All SECURITY DEFINER with search_path=pg_catalog,pg_temp and fully qualified types/objects/operators/functions. Every scalar return is pg_catalog.bytea, non-null, exactly one row; its closed tuple schema is below. S04 instead returns TABLE(installed_rank pg_catalog.int4,version pg_catalog.text,type pg_catalog.text,script pg_catalog.text,checksum pg_catalog.int4,success pg_catalog.bool), 0..N rows ordered by installed_rank, no truncation. Version/checksum may be null to preserve malformed-history evidence; readiness rejects unexpected nulls.

ALL_ARGUMENTS_EXPLICIT_CAST_AT_JDBC_BOUNDARY=YES. Calls use `public.name(?::pg_catalog.uuid,?::pg_catalog.bytea,...)` for every argument from this manifest. Use typed UUID/long/int/bytes/string/timestamp binding; reject null/shape locally too. No bare unknown literals, implicit cast dependence, defaults, variadic or additional same-name overloads. Validate pg_proc input/output type vectors, names/modes, security/volatility and regprocedure exactly. Internal calls also cast explicitly. GRANT/DROP/ALTER tooling always uses the full qualified input vector, never function name alone.

S15 outcomes exactly DELIVERY_ACKNOWLEDGED/DELIVERY_FAILED_REVIEW_REQUIRED/DELIVERY_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN; observation codes COMPLETE/PARTIAL/IO_FAILURE/PROCESS_UNCERTAINTY. Observation time is evidence only. Receipt UUIDs never possession.

All bytea request/return tuples use21.2, domain FLOOOW/OFFLINE-FIELD-PROOF/{Sxx}/{INPUT|OUTPUT}/V1, except the explicitly specified S17/S18 unchanged-envelope relationship in22.2. Fields are typed, closed, sequential tags in exact lists below; no arbitrary dispatcher/JSON. Each request maps only to its static frozen capability. Header/manifest/operation/authority redundancies must match before lookup. Frozen expected snapshot fields must come from BEGIN in this same connection/transaction and are revalidated by frozen APPLY; S17/S18 transport is exclusively22.2; no SQL proof of JCA is claimed. Null input tuple fields forbidden except decision supersedes_decision_id/provider_revision_local where frozen domain permits. Output tuple preserves exactly frozen outcome-dependent SQL nulls with00 presence; absence never means zero/authority. All new scalar request-byte lengths and nested collection counts are checked against the frozen field bounds before decoding into SQL arguments. Frozen variadic transaction_identity_hash remains unchanged; internal calls supply an explicitly typed VARIADIC pg_catalog.text[] array, never ambiguous expanded unknown literals. The no-variadic rule concerns new public/internal surface signatures, not edits to frozen helpers. int4/int8/text/uuid/bytea/instant use21.2; char(n) exact existing validated ASCII bytes, no padding/coercion; civil timestamp without time zone signedi64 microseconds since civil1970-01-01 without timezone conversion.

| Signature | Closed typed output |
| --- | --- |
| S01 | Exact nine-field preflight frame+MAC in21.2 |
| S02 | binding_state text;attempt_state text-or-null;generation int8;execution_state text-or-null;delivery_state text;reconciliation_state text;ceremony_result text;domain_projection text;admission_state text-or-null;admission_effectively_valid bool;counts bytea |

| S14 | delivery_receipt_id uuid;credential_id uuid;initial_operation_id uuid;fresh_applied_receipt_id uuid;execution_id uuid;instance_id uuid;attempted_at timestamptz;operation_deadline timestamptz;permission_to_attempt bool |

counts domain FLOOOW/OFFLINE-FIELD-PROOF/COUNTS/V1 has seven nonnegative int8 tags: accepted,consumption,principal,credential-revisions,grant-revisions,authority-operations,decision/head pairs. Unexpected/missing counts visible. Domain projection section2 enum plus MISMATCH/UNKNOWN. Missing control rows are absent, never fabricated; generation initialized by ADMIN. S03 accepted_snapshot/decision_snapshot use full explicit column tuples in21.7; evidence_snapshot fields: marketplace_key text,promotion_outcome text,source_integration_ref text,omie_currency text,semantic_fingerprint_version int4,source_evidence_semantic_fingerprint text,provider_revision_local civil instant-or-null. Fixed nested domains FLOOOW/OFFLINE-FIELD-PROOF/RECONCILE-{ACCEPTED|DECISION|EVIDENCE}/V1. Missing essential projection=>INDETERMINATE; no SQL JCA claim; adapter JCA and separate independent administrative recording remain required. Outcome EXACT is a database structural result, not ceremony SUCCESS. A must enforce the current acceptedArtifact signer-key/authority joins,states,approval-source/action/role/permission and verified-at windows before providing an accepted snapshot as structurally exact. The same scope/count/cardinality predicates remain mandatory; output absence or mismatch cannot be hidden by successful JCA over other bytes.


### S13-S16 exact output transport closure

This closure expands the existing Control outputs rows above without adding fields or changing section8/21.5 semantics. Each successful wrapper returns exactly one non-null pg_catalog.bytea frame with domain FLOOOW/OFFLINE-FIELD-PROOF/{Sxx}/OUTPUT/V1. Apply section21.2 exactly: u32be(domain byte length)||domain||u16be(FIELD_COUNT), followed by each u16be(TAG)||u32be(payload byte length)||payload. ORDER equals TAG. No extra, omitted, duplicate, reordered or trailing fields. CANONICAL_ENCODING below describes the complete field payload, including its presence byte.

All listed fields have NULLABLE=NO: successful S13 requires a complete original claim; successful S14 requires the original consumed delivery record, including on repeat; successful S15 requires a complete recorded report; successful S16 requires an effectively usable ISSUED admission. Missing required evidence denies the operation through existing error handling; it is not a partial success tuple. Null is not zero, absent is not empty, and no null sentinel, empty substitute or fabricated timestamp/identity is permitted. No secret, verifier, possession digest or extra authority flag is returned.

WRAPPER=S13 public.offline_claim_attempt (exact S13 signature in21.1)
FIELD_COUNT=8

| TAG | NAME | TYPE | ORDER | NULLABLE | SEMANTIC_SOURCE | CANONICAL_ENCODING |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | attempt_id | pg_catalog.uuid | 1 | NO | Original server-created attempt identity; section8; existing21.1 Control outputs | 01 || 16 network-order UUID bytes |
| 2 | generation | pg_catalog.int8 | 2 | NO | Current claimed generation; original on acknowledgment retry; section8; existing21.1 Control outputs | 01 || signed 8-byte big-endian two’s-complement integer |
| 3 | execution_id | pg_catalog.uuid | 3 | NO | Original owned execution identity; section8; existing21.1 Control outputs | 01 || 16 network-order UUID bytes |
| 4 | instance_id | pg_catalog.uuid | 4 | NO | Original launcher instance owning that execution; section8; existing21.1 Control outputs | 01 || 16 network-order UUID bytes |
| 5 | claimed_at | pg_catalog.timestamptz | 5 | NO | Original attempt claim database timestamp; section8; existing21.1 Control outputs | 01 || signed int8 big-endian epoch microseconds; reject sub-microsecond input |
| 6 | attempt_expires_at | pg_catalog.timestamptz | 6 | NO | Original absolute attempt deadline; section8; existing21.1 Control outputs | 01 || signed int8 big-endian epoch microseconds; reject sub-microsecond input |
| 7 | execution_expires_at | pg_catalog.timestamptz | 7 | NO | Original absolute execution deadline; section8; existing21.1 Control outputs | 01 || signed int8 big-endian epoch microseconds; reject sub-microsecond input |
| 8 | claim_receipt_id | pg_catalog.uuid | 8 | NO | Original claim receipt identity; section8; existing21.1 Control outputs | 01 || 16 network-order UUID bytes |

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | delivery_receipt_id | pg_catalog.uuid | 1 | NO | Original durable delivery-attempt receipt identity; section8; existing21.1 Control outputs | 01 || 16 network-order UUID bytes |
