## 23. G3F.3A crypto dependency contract approval

DESIGN-AMENDMENT / NO-RUNTIME. This bounded normative section closes only the two crypto dependency contract blockers and supersedes their unresolved version/ownership/ACL reservations in21.7,22.3 and22.4. Four operational identities,18 public signatures, frozen domain capabilities, Q readiness guards, key lifecycle/storage and all other Package0090 contracts remain unchanged. CONTRACT_APPROVAL != INSTALLED_PROOF. No database connection, installation, role or privilege mutation is performed by this document.

### 23.1 Exact ownership, authority and HMAC contract

D is exact PostgreSQL role name postgres, required to act as trusted SCHEMA_DEPLOYMENT_ADMIN and own both crypto extensions, offline_crypto schema and all their member crypto functions. This specifies a future deployment requirement, not verified live existence/ownership. D is a trusted installation superuser, distinct from V/I/E/A/P/Q/Z, all four bound service identities and shared RUNTIME/EXECUTOR/verifier/issuer/governance identities. No protected role has membership or SET ROLE path to D. D belongs to the existing trusted administrative boundary, not a new operational or wrapper-owner class. Existing ADMIN control/key governance and its separately approved identity remain unchanged; this amendment does not assign controls or keys to D.

Approve PostgreSQL18 dependency pgcrypto extension version1.4, with member objects in offline_crypto, and flooow_offline_mac32 extension version1.0. Extensions themselves are database-wide objects with no schema and no SQL GRANT EXECUTE/USAGE; member privileges are separately governed. Installation is a separate deployment/admin transaction, executed directly as D superuser so extension and member owners are exactly D; do not silently accept a trusted non-superuser install with different bootstrap-superuser member ownership.

Approve only offline_crypto.hmac(pg_catalog.bytea,pg_catalog.bytea,pg_catalog.text) RETURNS pg_catalog.bytea for the canonical binary path: frame bytes, private key bytes, statically fixed sha256 text respectively. LANGUAGE C, IMMUTABLE, STRICT, SECURITY INVOKER; scalar/non-SETOF, no defaults/variadic, unnamed inputs, proconfig NULL. No text/text overload or caller-selected algorithm is approved. HMAC-SHA-256, exact32-byte MAC and nine-field FLOOOW/OFFLINE-FIELD-PROOF/PREFLIGHT/V1 frame stay21.2/22.3. Extension version1.4 is distinct from deployment key version and comparator version.

Q=flooow_offline_readiness_owner is the only non-owner HMAC/comparator EXECUTE grantee and crypto-schema USAGE grantee, without grant option/CREATE. D retains administrative owner rights as deployment authority. PUBLIC EXECUTE/USAGE/CREATE on the private surface is forbidden; no Package0090 role receives broad pgcrypto access. All other pgcrypto members, including the text HMAC overload, have no Package/PUBLIC non-owner EXECUTE. Forbidden identities include V/I/E/A/P/Z, bound VERIFIER/ISSUER/EXECUTOR/AUDITOR logins and shared runtime/verifier/issuer/governance roles; no direct, PUBLIC, default, owner or membership path may bypass this boundary.

Q keeps its accepted symmetric preflight MAC generation/verification duty, not domain evidence acceptance or authority issuance. V's evidence verification and I's principal/credential/grant issuance remain independent; E's decision execution and normal RUNTIME do not acquire signing/key authority. A/E invoke the existing guarded Q readiness capability and receive bounded receipt bytes only, never key material or a generic MAC oracle. No plaintext key storage or key retrieval by RUNTIME/EXECUTOR is allowed, including application files/memory/logs, raw SQL access and function returns. Accepted private ADMIN-governed offline_preflight_key storage/Q-only five-column reads in22.3/22.4 are retained, not redesigned into encryption-at-rest/KMS. ADMIN alone provisions/rotates/purges; Q reads current ACTIVE32-byte material internally; services/A/E never retrieve it. No new export/decrypt/key-retrieval object is approved. Trusted administrative superuser bypass stays outside the operational attacker model.

### 23.2 Exact timing-safe SQL signature and semantics

Approve offline_crypto.timing_safe_equal32(pg_catalog.bytea,pg_catalog.bytea) RETURNS pg_catalog.bool with two unnamed mandatory inputs, no defaults/variadic, scalar/non-SETOF; LANGUAGE C, IMMUTABLE, STRICT, PARALLEL SAFE, SECURITY INVOKER, proconfig NULL. Module=$libdir/flooow_offline_mac32; C symbol=timing_safe_equal32. This is a private dependency, not a nineteenth public wrapper.

Preserve source semantics exactly: either SQL NULL returns SQL NULL without invoking C, including when the other argument has invalid length. For two non-NULL inputs each has exactly32 payload bytes; source checks raw datum size before detoasting and payload length afterwards. Invalid length raises SQLSTATE22023/invalid_parameter_value, source message Invalid MAC length. CRYPTO_memcmp compares exactly32 bytes, true iff equal, false otherwise. No padding/truncation/text coercion/alternative signature is approved. Q first rejects invalid framing, unknown/inactive key version and malformed/missing/non32-byte MACs; accepts only comparison result IS TRUE. NULL never authenticates. Existing wrapper diagnostic sanitization stays mandatory.

OWNER=D=postgres; non-owner EXECUTE only Q without grant option; PUBLIC EXECUTE forbidden. Direct/effective RUNTIME/EXECUTOR/E/A/V/I/P/Z/service EXECUTE forbidden. Q invokes only for preflight MAC verification under21.3/22.3; no comparator access is added to E credential/admission/possession paths. Deployment installs/audits; Q verifies; A preflight and E claim use Q's existing bound caller/plan guard.

Bind comparator approval to supplied prior evidence: SO_SHA256=61347c5ad692a5e73664ee9a59907576a7ebde9ecdd24edc92fff237c15a11a7; PostgreSQL=18.4; OpenSSL=3.5.6; CRYPTO_memcmp=CRYPTO_memcmp@@OPENSSL_3.0.0. This gate does not rehash/install the binary. That identity covers the comparator only, never pgcrypto. pgcrypto package/binary provenance, exact installed ownership/ACLs, golden/RNG and other existing implementation gates remain mandatory.

### 23.3 One atomic ownership/ACL/default-ACL manifest

The table is normative. D=postgres; V/I/E/A/P/Q/Z retain exact21.7 names; bound login means immutable header OID/name, never a shared role. Owner implicit rights are separately included, never operational grants. Every non-owner grant is without grant option. All unlisted grantees/privileges are forbidden; no wildcard/name-only grant is permitted. DF below is the DEFAULT_ACL_REQUIREMENT for every row:

Actual creation principals, not merely final owners, must be inventoried: postgres, V/I/E/A/P/Q/Z exact21.7 names and existing approved ADMIN/control/frozen creators. Enumerate global/public/offline_crypto and all actual additional scopes for the six22.1 object kinds. New function creators require effective owner-only default EXECUTE: remove global PUBLIC EXECUTE before creation; schema-local revoke cannot cancel global/hard-wired PUBLIC defaults. No default non-owner EXECUTE, including Q, or operational schema CREATE/relation/sequence/grant-option access. Exact allowed grants are explicit after creation. Private schema defaults confer no PUBLIC/non-owner USAGE/CREATE. Changing defaults is not retroactive: current PUBLIC/unlisted ACLs and extension-script grants also require closure. Frozen ownership/ACLs remain unchanged; unsafe existing creator paths require separately governed deployment remediation, never silent V043 repair. Owner implicit rights remain projected.

Private HMAC integration is exactly the pgcrypto binary HMAC dependency and existing Q-owned public.offline_internal_readiness body. Its existing ADMIN-owned key-table dependency and Q SELECT(incarnation_id,lineage_id,key_version,key_state,key_material) remain22.4, with no new key/table grant or ownership reassignment. No extra MAC/export/verification function or operational role is created. All18 consumer signatures above are individually enumerated. P/Z and frozen dependencies remain21.7/22.4 unchanged.

Deployment atomicity: operational exclusion/NOT_READY; verify D creator/owner and safe private schema; version-pin/install both extensions in a separate administrative transaction; verify all member owners; close DF and every current PUBLIC/unlisted member ACL; grant only Q schema USAGE and two exact crypto EXECUTE signatures; commit only the complete approved state. Any mismatch rolls back and remains NOT_READY. No operational exposure between extension creation and ACL closure. Existing different schema/version/owner/ACL installations fail closed for separately governed remediation, never silent relocation/adoption or normal-runtime grant changes.

V043 MUST NOT CREATE EXTENSION, install/load crypto binaries or repair crypto owner/ACL/defaults. V043 may depend on approved objects being present and fail closed on absence/mismatch; it may create Package0090 controls/wrappers and their exact unchanged consumer ACLs in its own atomic migration transaction. Installation != runtime permission; separate deployment prerequisites are not supplied by PUBLIC. Deployment must record complete exact pgcrypto1.4 member/creator inventory and real catalog evidence before activation, never trust live extras as approved.

DEPLOYMENT != VERIFIER != ISSUER != RUNTIME != EXECUTOR.
CAPABILITY != AUTHORITY. INSTALLATION != RUNTIME_PERMISSION. PUBLIC != PACKAGE_0090_AUTHORITY.
PGCRYPTO_CONTRACT_APPROVED=YES;TIMING_SAFE_CONTRACT_APPROVED=YES;CRYPTO_OWNERSHIP_APPROVED=YES;CRYPTO_ACL_APPROVED=YES;DEFAULT_ACL_CONTRACT_APPROVED=YES;DEPLOYMENT_RUNTIME_SEPARATION=PASS;UNRESOLVED_BLOCKER_COUNT=0 for these two contract blockers.
CRYPTO_DEPENDENCY_CONTRACT_APPROVAL=PASS;V043_IMPLEMENTATION_READY=YES for the next bounded contract-scoped implementation task;NEXT_GATE=G3F.3B_V043_IMPLEMENTATION_AND_CONTRACT_CLOSURE. No runtime/activation/field-proof authorization: REAL_FIELD_PROOF=HOLD. Historical broader independent review and actual implementation evidence requirements remain in force.

## 24. ADMIN identity and attribute contract closure

DESIGN-AMENDMENT / OFFLINE / FAIL-CLOSED. This section resolves the separately approved ADMIN identity reference in21.7,22.1 and23.1. It approves design only; no installed posture is asserted. The ownership identity is not the human/host administrative responsibilities called ADMIN elsewhere. Those governed lifecycle/key actions remain performed by trusted administration, not an interactive connection as this role.

### 24.1 Exact identity, attributes and separation

ADMIN_ROLE_NAME=flooow_offline_control_owner. Production IDENTITY purpose12 resolves this exact role name and live OID; LOGIN=false, SUPERUSER=false, CREATEDB=false, CREATEROLE=false, REPLICATION=false, BYPASSRLS=false, INHERIT=true. No password/provider credential is provisioned to this NOLOGIN role; it is not directly usable for interactive login. It is neither deployment login nor VERIFIER/ISSUER/RUNTIME/EXECUTOR/AUDITOR/application principal. No replication, bypass, operational execution or canonical domain authority is granted by this approval.

Require zero incoming and outgoing membership edges, including transitive inheritance/SET ROLE paths. No operational login, shared role or wrapper/internal owner may inherit, SET ROLE to, or receive ownership through ADMIN. ADMIN inherits no external authority despite INHERIT=true. Trusted postgres deployment authority remains outside the operational attacker model and uses its existing superuser boundary, not an added membership. Existing zero-membership/NOINHERIT rules for services and V/I/E/A/P/Q/Z are unchanged and do not apply NOINHERIT to ADMIN.

The conceptual ACL codec fixtures in22.1 use synthetic role admin, LOGIN=true/INHERIT=false, schema/database ownership and fixture grants solely to define test bytes. They are not the production ADMIN expectation, cannot authorize those attributes/ownership/grants for flooow_offline_control_owner, and remain distinct from production readiness approval. No fixture bytes or golden values are silently reinterpreted.

### 24.2 Closed ownership and ACL scope

ADMIN may own only these exact Package0090 tables, with exactly the existing21.7 column schemas and22.3 key rules:

- public.offline_binding_header
- public.offline_binding_lifecycle
- public.offline_attempt_pointer
- public.offline_attempt
- public.offline_execution
- public.offline_delivery
- public.offline_admission
- public.offline_stage_receipt
- public.offline_reconciliation
- public.offline_ceremony_result
- public.offline_readiness
- public.offline_preflight_key
- public.offline_deadline_policy
- public.offline_diagnostic_evidence

Only intrinsic indexes, constraints and automatically associated composite row types belonging to those tables accompany this ownership. No standalone sequence/type, function, extension, schema, database, frozen domain table or other object is added to the ownership set. Unexpected ownership fails precondition/readiness; no wildcard future ownership. V/I/E/A/P/Q/Z own only their existing approved functions. postgres remains owner of pgcrypto1.4, flooow_offline_mac32 extension1.0, offline_crypto and its crypto functions; no transfer to ADMIN is approved.

Owner implicit table rights are included in the ACL projection and are confined to the tables above. No broad schema/table privilege or schema CREATE is granted to ADMIN; ordinary public schema USAGE needed for its enumerated objects is the only application-schema allowance. No new database grant, crypto-schema access, frozen/internal/wrapper EXECUTE or grant-option delegation is approved. PUBLIC has no control/key privilege or membership through ADMIN. Non-owner operational capabilities remain only the existing explicit exact object/column grants of22.4 and exact signature grants of21.1/23.3; service logins retain wrapper-only access. This amendment adds no grant row, helper, export, key-retrieval capability or autonomous administrative execution.

### 24.3 Default ACL and actual creator requirements

Inventory ADMIN by its exact name in the DEFAULT creator projection and inventory postgres and every actual creation principal separately under23.3. Ownership transfer retains creation-time ACLs; it neither applies ADMIN defaults nor closes PUBLIC/unlisted grants. Independently verify current ACLs on every transferred object before operational exposure.

For ADMIN, require no non-owner default privilege or grant option in global, public, offline_crypto or any actual additional scope across all six22.1 object kinds. No default PUBLIC table/column/sequence access, function EXECUTE, schema USAGE/CREATE, type USAGE or large-object access may confer Package0090 authority. PostgreSQL hard-wired defaults are not authority approval: global effective default PUBLIC function EXECUTE and type USAGE must be removed in separately governed provisioning before any such ADMIN creation; schema-local revocation alone cannot cancel global defaults. Empty/no-entry catalog representation is validated against the PG18 hard-wired defaults, not treated as automatically safe. Preserve owner implicit privileges and PostgreSQL intrinsic object creation behavior; reject any remaining PUBLIC/non-owner operational access. Explicit approved object/column grants are applied only after creation, never through defaults.

No object creation as ADMIN is authorized by this amendment. Controls are created/transferred by postgres deployment authority, with that actual creator's defaults and current ACLs independently closed under23.3. Unsafe existing defaults/current ACLs require separate governed remediation; V043 must fail closed instead of silently repairing them. Default posture is contract approval, not live proof or permission to change frozen objects.

### 24.4 Provisioning and V043 authority

ADMIN_ROLE_CREATION_OWNER=SEPARATE_ADMINISTRATIVE_PROVISIONING;V043_ADMIN_ROLE_CREATION_AUTHORIZED=NO. Trusted deployment administration provisions the exact NOLOGIN role, attributes, membership exclusion and default posture separately while operational access remains excluded/NOT_READY. V043 must treat its presence and approved posture as mandatory preconditions. Missing role, wrong attributes, memberships, unexpected ownership/privileges/defaults or unsafe creator paths cause failure, not adoption/automatic repair. No V043 CREATE ROLE, ALTER ROLE, membership grant or default-ACL repair for ADMIN is authorized.

After successful preconditions, the future migration under postgres deployment authority may create the exact listed controls and transfer their ownership to ADMIN atomically with existing exact non-owner ACLs. ADMIN is not required to obtain CREATE, login or deployment ownership to accept that transfer. This is the narrow ownership authority only; no changes to crypto installation/ACL/defaults or frozen domains are permitted. The existing V043 draft's NOINHERIT role creation is unapproved source and must be replaced by the precondition in G3F.3B; no draft change occurs in this design-only task.

ADMIN_IDENTITY_APPROVED=YES;ADMIN_ROLE_NAME=flooow_offline_control_owner;ADMIN_LOGIN=NO;ADMIN_SUPERUSER=NO;ADMIN_BYPASSRLS=NO;ADMIN_ROLE_SEPARATION=PASS;ADMIN_OWNERSHIP_SCOPE_CLOSED=YES;ADMIN_ACL_SCOPE_CLOSED=YES;UNRESOLVED_BLOCKER_COUNT=0 for the ADMIN identity contract blocker.
ADMIN_IDENTITY_AND_ATTRIBUTE_CONTRACT_CLOSURE=PASS;V043_IMPLEMENTATION_READY=YES for bounded G3F.3B implementation;NEXT_GATE=G3F.3B_V043_IMPLEMENTATION_AND_CONTRACT_CLOSURE;REAL_FIELD_PROOF=HOLD. Migration execution, installed role/ACL/catalog proof, launcher and field-proof gates remain unauthorized by this amendment.

Technical reference verification: PostgreSQL18 pgcrypto.control on REL_18_STABLE specifies default_version1.4; PostgreSQL18 CREATE EXTENSION defines creator ownership and trusted-extension bootstrap ownership. These inform deployment mechanics, not Package0090 authority:
https://raw.githubusercontent.com/postgres/postgres/REL_18_STABLE/contrib/pgcrypto/pgcrypto.control
https://www.postgresql.org/docs/18/sql-createextension.html

## 25. Offline policy bootstrap contract closure

DESIGN-AMENDMENT / OFFLINE / FAIL-CLOSED. This section supersedes the former SPEC18 and companion ADR precondition requiring policy values before V043 execution. All existing role, crypto, creator/default-ACL, schema-safety and migration preconditions remain independent. Missing policy values do not prevent creation of their storage; they prevent policy-dependent operational use. This amendment changes contracts only and performs no provisioning or migration.

### 25.1 Schema creation and explicit bootstrap state

V043_POLICY_SCHEMA_CREATION_AUTHORIZED=YES. V043 may create the approved policy storage/schema objects and transfer ownership under24. It may add effective_from pg_catalog.timestamptz(6) NOT NULL, with no DEFAULT, to public.offline_deadline_policy as immutable activation metadata. No policy row, numeric TTL/maximum, production policy_version, effective_from value, readiness activation or policy value provisioning is authorized inside V043. V043_POLICY_VALUE_PROVISIONING_AUTHORIZED=NO. No empty substitute or synthetic policy satisfies this contract.

POLICY_BOOTSTRAP_STATE=UNPROVISIONED | ACTIVE is a derived state for the exact required policy_version/policy_digest, not a new persisted status column/table or public flag. It does not add a wrapper/helper or alter the18 public signatures.

- UNPROVISIONED: no committed independently approved exact row, missing required field/approval/audit evidence, invalid canonical bytes/digest/bounds, scope/reference mismatch, or database clock before effective_from. Missing/invalid policy fails closed; it is not assumed to be a zero or default policy.
- ACTIVE: the explicit administrative provisioning transaction is committed and auditable; its complete immutable row passes25.3, exact required version/digest references match, and fresh pg_catalog.clock_timestamp() >= effective_from. ACTIVE is necessary for policy-dependent wrapper use; every existing R/M/P/Q/Z, READY/watchdog, binding, lifecycle, transaction, possession, admission and frozen-domain guard remains required.

All18 Package0090 wrappers and P/Q/Z require the bound policy for their existing read/mutation/readiness guards and therefore deny policy-dependent use while UNPROVISIONED, including auditor read/preflight and claim retries. Empty policy storage is a permitted post-schema bootstrap condition, never operational readiness. Policy failure uses existing sanitized fail-closed diagnostics; no partial success receipt, status repair or inferred validity. No fallback TTL, session setting/config/GUC, hidden default, caller-supplied TTL, transaction duration substitution or client clock is a policy source.

### 25.2 Separate administrative authority and exact sequence

POLICY_PROVISIONING_AUTHORITY=TRUSTED_DEPLOYMENT_ADMIN_POSTGRES_UNDER_EXPLICIT_ADMIN_POLICY_GOVERNANCE. The trusted administrative operator authenticates directly as the existing postgres deployment authority and performs the separately approved control-policy provisioning transaction. ADMIN=flooow_offline_control_owner remains the NOLOGIN owner in24; it receives no credential, membership, SET ROLE route, schema CREATE, wrapper execution or autonomous administrative API. This is trusted administrative table provisioning, not a service capability. V/I/E/A/P/Q/Z, bound service identities, shared RUNTIME/EXECUTOR/verifier/issuer/governance roles and callers cannot provision, select an alternate policy, activate or rotate it. No new role, function or table is introduced.

Exact sequence:

1. V043 schema creation under the existing independently satisfied deployment/role/crypto/schema/default-ACL preconditions; policy storage is initially empty and POLICY_BOOTSTRAP_STATE=UNPROVISIONED.
2. Separate ADMIN provisioning after successful schema creation: obtain independently approved policy values, approval identity and activation time; begin a distinct administrative transaction under operational exclusion/NOT_READY, using existing control serialization for any affected binding/readiness rows.
3. Policy validation: insert the exact new immutable policy row and validate25.3 before commit; establish the existing exact policy-version/digest binding/readiness references when those rows are provisioned. Record durable audit evidence under25.4. Failure rolls back; no wrapper use is permitted during this transaction. An unreferenced policy row never supplies binding authority.
4. ACTIVE: only after acknowledged administrative commit, complete approval/audit evidence and successful validation, and only when database wall clock reaches effective_from. Future activation time keeps the required policy UNPROVISIONED for operational eligibility until then. Deployment READY and binding activation require their separate existing evidence; they are not automatic effects of policy insertion.
5. Wrapper use permitted only for the exact ACTIVE bound policy and after all remaining existing guards pass. A wrapper independently reads/validates the bound policy and activation time; it never trusts an external ACTIVE assertion or session parameter.

V043 must not execute steps2-4 or administrative policy provisioning. A future authorization to change that boundary would require an explicit ADR/SPEC amendment; none is granted here. No production numeric policy values or production version are selected by this amendment.

### 25.3 Exact row, digest and binding validation

The administrative authority supplies policy_version pg_catalog.text, canonical_policy pg_catalog.bytea, policy_digest pg_catalog.bytea and effective_from pg_catalog.timestamptz(6), all required and immutable. policy_version is the exact independently approved nonempty NFC identity. canonical_policy is the complete exact29-tag DEADLINE-POLICY/V1 frame from21.2; preflight_receipt_ttl and its approved maximum are positive finite int8 microseconds at tags28/29, actual<=maximum. Every other actual/maximum pair, nesting, finite bound and overflow rule remains mandatory. policy_digest is exactly32 bytes and equals pg_catalog.sha256(canonical_policy), and must match the independently approved digest; neither live bytes alone nor a caller digest establishes approval. Tag1 exactly equals row policy_version. Reject malformed, missing, duplicate, reordered, trailing, incomplete27-field or unapproved policy bytes.

Effective_from is an independently approved finite database instant with microsecond precision, supplied explicitly in the same provisioning transaction; reject null/infinity or unsupported precision. It is immutable activation metadata for that policy version, not issued_at, not a receipt TTL and not an additional canonical-policy tag. The29-field policy and nine-field receipt encodings remain unchanged. The independent approval/audit record binds effective_from to the exact version, complete canonical bytes and digest. Never backfill/default this field or change it in place. A changed effective_from, TTL, maximum or any other policy value requires a newly approved policy_version and digest under25.5; even where identical canonical values yield identical digest, activation metadata cannot be rebound to an old version.

Existing public.offline_binding_header.deadline_policy_version/deadline_policy_digest and public.offline_readiness.policy_version/policy_digest must reference that exact approved row before the corresponding binding can be used. Preserve all existing canonical binding/fingerprint, independently expected policy digest, incarnation, surface and watchdog checks; policy bootstrap never invents binding values or silently adopts server data. Provisioning references follows existing administrative binding/readiness governance, never wrapper DML or implicit activation. Recompute/reapprove dependent policy/binding manifests and golden evidence when required.

The21.7 control inventory includes effective_from. The22.4 additions are exactly READ_PRIVILEGE (SQL column SELECT) on public.offline_deadline_policy.effective_from for V/I/E/A/P/Q/Z, each solely for its existing bound-policy guard. These seven rows are enumerated in22.4. No new operational table access, DML grant, key/crypto privilege, object ownership, function EXECUTE or helper is authorized. Include the column and its exact grants in the approved ACL projection/digest before operational readiness. Each existing guard uses the fresh database clock for activation; session configuration cannot override it.

### 25.4 Transactional and auditable provisioning

Provisioning is explicit, separate from V043, atomic and governed by a prior independent administrative approval. One transaction inserts the new policy row and, where applicable, atomically establishes its existing approved references under the existing lifecycle/control locks. It validates the complete row/reference set before commit. No in-place policy UPDATE/DELETE, upsert that overwrites a version, default insertion, unapproved binding mutation or reuse of an existing version is permitted. Operational exclusion prevents exposure of tentative/intermediate state. Validation/query/commit uncertainty denies activation until durable committed state and audit evidence are independently reconciled; no retry may replace values under the same version.

A durable trusted administrative provisioning record outside the operational wrapper surface must contain: approval/change identity, administrative actor, target deployment/incarnation, policy_version, complete canonical_policy bytes and policy_digest, effective_from, affected binding IDs and exact version/digest references where applicable, validation outcome, transaction/commit identity and acknowledged outcome with database timestamps. Prepare the record before the transaction, associate the exact transaction with it, then record confirmed commit or rollback/unknown outcome. Missing or uncertain audit/commit evidence keeps deployment NOT_READY and wrapper use denied until independently reconciled. This existing trusted deployment audit obligation authorizes no new Package0090 audit table, helper, operational API or repository artifact. It contains no key/verifier/possession/credential material. Bookkeeping and audit evidence do not replace policy approval or other canonical authority.

### 25.5 Receipt immutability and rotation

Every issued receipt remains bound to its original exact immutable policy_version through its policy digest and binding, and retains original issued_at, expires_at and MAC. Q derives expiry with that policy's preflight_receipt_ttl using21.2/21.3, never a later policy or changed activation time. No receipt renewal, expiry rewrite, fallback or grace period is introduced.

Rotation is a new separately approved administrative provisioning transaction for a new immutable policy_version with complete canonical bytes/digest and effective_from, under operational exclusion and existing binding/readiness governance. Old policy rows remain immutable and retained; old bindings are never rebound or rewritten. Provision new governed bindings where a new policy requires changed immutable binding fields. Readiness changes retain existing exclusion/drain and independent expected-manifest requirements. Rotation cannot silently adopt a new version for a running attempt or extend its execution/admission/delivery deadlines.

Validation of an existing receipt still requires the exact original policy to match the current approved binding/readiness under21.3. If rotation causes that match to fail, reject the old receipt; do not rederive expiry with the new TTL or issue replacement bytes on the executor route. Receipt immutability does not guarantee continued acceptance after policy/readiness drift. Missing, invalid, not-yet-effective or mismatched policy always fails closed. Fresh S01 issuance is a separate eligible operation and cannot revive old receipt/execution/delivery/admission authority.
